# Open threads register

> **Generated** from the escalation table (type=thread) by `Escalation.ps1 -Action Export -Type thread`. Do not edit by hand: respond through `Escalation.ps1 -Action Update -Id OT-nn` (escalation #2000). The hand-kept register (2026-10-02 to 2026-10-12) is frozen at `archive/open-threads-register-frozen-20261012.md`.

**Set up:** 2026-10-02 (researcher: *"I approve of the open threads register"*, #1935). Carried in the escalation table from 2026-10-12 (#2000).

**How it is used** (`cfg_behaviour_rule` open-threads-carried-as-escalations): The open threads register is carried in the escalation table as type='thread' (alias OT-nn), not edited as a markdown file. A thread is not worked as a task: it moves only when a strand reaches it (focused capture, rule 84). Its items (escalation_thread_item) are registered and reworded through Open-Item.ps1, each to one read purpose (rule B:115: a face or perspective, its target scoped to the verses and strongs of that purpose, what to read for, why it is open, when it comes back); items of the older kinds (cluster, Strong's, verse, strand, chapter, event, researcher, escalation) are reworded to that form. Items are what a strand, task or reading is matched against (Escalation.ps1 -Action Match; a worded item on its target verses). Claude manages each thread's lifecycle; the researcher gives feedback in chat, which Claude records verbatim the same turn, and there is no approval step, for a thread or for any item. Every response to an item -- touched, narrowed, forward, ruling, note, closed, reopened, withdrawn, superseded -- is an escalation_thread_item_response row, recorded through Escalation.ps1 -Action Update -Id OT-nn -Item ... -ResponseKind ... -Respond ..., and written in full to the thread's history. A thread completes when its last open item is settled; a thread is withdrawn or superseded (-SupersededBy OT-nn) with its open items settled in the same call. Notes and comments on a thread are Updates, never edits to the register file. The file _analytics/cross-cluster-web/open-threads-register.md is generated (Escalation.ps1 -Action Export -Type thread) and regenerated at session-close when a thread row changed in the session (SC12). Its quality, validity and integrity are audited at every start (Escalation.ps1 -Action ThreadAudit). Before every unit pull of a strand, the threads that bear on it are matched (Escalation.ps1 -Action Match -Cluster .. -Strong .. -Verse ..; -For nnnn records the match); held items and researcher questions are raised as threads at every ledger approval (Escalation.ps1 -Action Raise -Type thread).

**Threads:** completed 19, in-progress 1, on-hold 19, supersede 69 (108 in all). Item references are `OT-nn.n`.

| OT-97 · #2098 | **M18 forward marks: verses where the M18 word only qualifies another cluster's characteristic.** From the role triage of the 94 remaining M18 group B bucket-1 verses (`../Clusters/M18 - desire-longing/m18-group-B-remaining-role-triage-v2-20261011.csv`, decision `forward` / `forward-carried`; reading `m18-group-B-remaining-role-reading-v1-20261011.md` §3). 43 verses, plus Hos 8:8 (44). In each, the M18 word (delight, pleasure, "seems good") is a qualifier, such as a measure, a ground or an object, of a characteristic carried by another M-code in the verse. The row names the main operation and what the M18 word gives it. Researcher, verbatim 2026-10-11: *"if it is supportive, for another cluster's reading then the reading is marked forward for when that cluster is read"*. Read with the guide `Workflow/Instructions/wa-verse-reading-by-role-guide-v1-20261011.md` (rule 73). **Group C (will, willing; 2026-10-12):** 31 more verses in `../Clusters/M18 - desire-longing/m18-group-C-role-triage-v2-20261012.csv` (decision `forward`): the will word qualifies another cluster's characteristic, for example "called by the will of God" (M42), "desiring to justify himself" (M26), "I am unwilling to send them away hungry" (M50), "Rachel refused to be comforted" (M03), and Est 9:5 (M01). 2Th 3:10 waits for the strand that reads work (no M-code). **M18 × M72 shared verses (2026-10-12):** 23 more in `../Clusters/M18 - desire-longing/m18-m72-shared-triage-v2-20261012.csv` (decision `forward`; reading `m18-m72-shared-reading-v1-20261012.md` §6), for example the ground of a king's hatred, *"he never prophesies good concerning me"* (1Ki 22:8; M06, M43), *"speak favorably"* (1Ki 22:13; M65), and *"for the Lord is good"* (Jer 33:11; M49). The seven that go to M72 itself are on OT-98. **M72 group B (2026-10-12, #1998):** 47 more in `../Clusters/M72 - authority-dominion/m72-group-B-role-triage-v1-20261012.csv` (decision `forward`). In each, rule or authority is only the setting, outcome or ground of another cluster's act. Examples: the people rejoice or groan under their rulers (Pro 29:2), rule as the outcome of diligence (Pro 12:24), and the crowds astonished at his authority (Mat 7:29). | OT-98 · #2099 | **M72 (Authority & Dominion) and M18 (Desire & Longing): 182 shared verses, with M18 readings to build on.** All 182 are now decided in the M18 strand (`../Clusters/M18 - desire-longing/m18-m72-shared-triage-v2-20261012.csv`; reading `m18-m72-shared-reading-v1-20261012.md`). 131 were decided earlier in the M18 read-backs and gloss groups; their decisions are in the CSV column `home_or_forward`, and only the group B-remaining and group C triages recorded the M72 word's role. The 51 never read were read by role on 2026-10-12. **Seven go forward to M72 itself**, because the M72 word carries the act: Gen 31:29 (the power to harm, held back by God's word, 31:24); Ezr 7:9, 8:18, Neh 2:8, 2:18 ("the good hand of my God", named as the reason for what happened); 1Sa 15:28 (the kingdom torn away); Isa 52:7 ("Your God reigns"). Pointer: 1Ki 2:15 ("the kingdom was mine"). | OT-99 · #2100 | **Fragmented verse readings across strands: an input for the segment re-read.** The M72 check of the completed strands (M25, M47, M18, M01, M02; 832 verses) found, in a test of 8, four readings that kept the strand's own clause and lost what the verse joins to it: 1Sa 7:3 (10.7 keeps "direct your heart", loses "he will deliver you out of the hand of the Philistines"); Mal 1:13 ("you snort at it", without "I am a great King", 1:14); Mic 4:4 (Ch 14 stops before "for the mouth of the Lord of hosts has spoken"); Jer 15:4 (FE-86 lists the people's deeds; the verse names "what Manasseh … king of Judah, did"). Researcher, verbatim: *"the triage of completed strands is pointing towards the existence of a deeper problem in the narrative - largely related to verse reading being fragmented accross different reading sessions … Downstream I will introduce a reread in any case of each segment of the narrative … it may be better to re-assess these complex context reading scenarios at that stage."* Inputs: `../Clusters/M72 - authority-dominion/m72-completed-strand-check-v1-20261012.csv` (832 rows, partner strands, narrative places) and `m72-completed-strand-check-test-v2-20261012.md`.<br>OT-99: item 2 (scope:1Sa 7:3; | OT-100 · #2101 | **The inner-being meaning of authority and ruling: not yet explored to the full.** The M72 group B reading (#1998) read 81 verses in 9 groups. The researcher's reading of it, verbatim: *"I don't think we really have discovered, debated or explored to the full the true meaning of authority and ruling from a inner being perspective.  M72 have added to the understanding, but maybe we are looking at it from the wrong direction … rule, authority, giving or having power is more of a state or status that works in collaboration with other characteristics and parties, but this is not yet strongly emerging from the material you are providing. I am not discounting that you are biased in your treatment of readings, so it is likely that I will need to read it again at some point."* Also: *"I dont' think the title of ch_11 is true to its contents"*. The current thoughts are in Ch 11 §7 *Authority as a standing* (v30), held as a first reading with a working note. Inputs: `../Clusters/M72 - authority-dominion/m72-group-B-reading-v1-20261012.md` (in it, the B word is often labelled the characteristic where another act carries the verse; see `m72-group-B-weave-record-v1-20261012.md` §2), the triage CSV, the passages file.<br>OT-100: item 1 (scope:Eph 6:12 and 80 more verses) note (via #1998) -- note by Claude: reworded (#2236); was: cluster:M72 | M72 group B weave (#1998, 2026-10-12) | the researcher re-reads the M72 material, or a later strand reads authority together with the characteristics that move inside it (fear, pride, wanting, care for another); the Ch 11 / §7 title question is answered | **on-hold**<br>OT-100.1 scope Eph 6:12 and 80 more verses (authority and ruling from the inner being: rule, authority, power as a state or status that works with other characteristics and parties (fear, pride, wanting, care for another), read again in the 81 verses of the M72 group B reading) -- open; latest: note (2026-10-23, #1998): reworded (#2236); was: cluster:M72<br>History before migration (from the register's status cell):<br>open; title answered 2026-10-12 (researcher: *"Ch 11 as a whole — the title should not carry any weight in the analysis, it is almost a collection of different inner being elements that does not have another home"*): Ch 11 renamed *Other sides of the inner being* (v31) and its opening restated. The meaning of authority stays open |

| OT-104 · #2145 | From the OT-02 die/death check (2026-10-14, check v2 section C, C24): in face D04 (struck down by God), these name an inner act and were not woven in Ch 2: Rev 2:23 (God 'searches mind and heart' as he strikes), 1Ch 10:14 (Saul 'did not seek guidance from the Lord. | OT-106 · #2150 | Researcher, verbatim 2026-10-15 (#2148): "poor - to be poor, lacking resources, is a state or status naming a persons availability of external resources (wealth);  it could also be a inner being characteristic where being or perceiving in the inner being of lack perceived but may not be true or could be true, but the verification is not in external resources, this characteristic is closely aligned to needy, although needy is an expression with an objective to get simpathy or help.  This description for you need to be corroborated in the verses, but I am highlighting that poor, needy have multiple dimensions". The 48 M09 verses where the verse puts the poor person first (state, standing, voice, act), plus H6041. The question carried: do the verses bear out the three dimensions (state of means; inner lack, perceived or real; needy as an appeal for help)? The 27 no-other-M-code verses showed only the first. Picked up when the clusters named are worked.<br>OT-106: item 12 strong:H6035 added<br>OT-106: item 3 (cluster:M47) note (via #2160) -- note by Claude: Read 2026-10-18 (#2160): Mat 5:3, Pro 16:19, Psa 109:22 are told, but not on OT-106's question. The passages corroborate the researcher's three dimensions: means (Exo 30:15, 2Sa 12:3), inner state not matching means (Mat 5:3; Pro 13:7 'pretends to be poor, yet has great wealth'), needy as appeal (Psa 109:22-26, 34:6).
